Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the affine framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 395. The resulting operator achieves bound O(737/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to affine structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 395/737 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^530(S, F) is trivial. QED.
